# React Native SDK Overview

The Appier Enterprise Service SDK ("Appier SDK") is the main SDK for Appier's Enterprise Products: AIXON, AIQUA, and AiDeal.

Refer to the following versions when setting up React Native integrations in AIQUA.

Current Appier SDK Version| SDK Download  
---|---  
**2.1.4**| [Appier React Native SDK](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@appier/react-native-sdk)  
  
> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> IMPORTANT NOTICES
> 
>   * If you're upgrading from version 1.8.0 or earlier, you'll need to change the React Native SDK package name from `react-native-aiqua-sdk` to `@appier/react-native-sdk` in all the import lines in your app and your app's dependencies in `package.json`
> 
>   * If you're setting your Android app's `targetSdkVersion` to `31` (Android 12), you'll need to upgrade to React Native SDK 1.8.0.
> 
>   * Starting in November 2021, Android app updates will be required to target API level 30 or above and adjust for behavioral changes in Android 11. See the official Android Developer guides [here](https://developer.android.com/distribute/best-practices/develop/target-sdk#pre11).  
>  **[SDK Upgrade Required]** To avoid a crash issue in Android apps targeting API level 30 or above, upgrade your app to Appier React Native SDK 1.6.1. See [Release Notes](doc:react-native-sdk-release-notes#v161---september-7-2021).
> 
>   * Starting from version 1.4.0, Appier SDK uses CocoaPods dependency. We recommend you to upgrade to React Native version > 0.60.
> 
>   * **If you are upgrading to Appier SDK 1.4.0 or above for the first time** , some migration steps are needed for iOS Rich Push integration. Refer to this [**Migration Doc**](doc:ios-sdk-migration-for-react-native).
> 
>   * Appier SDK **does NOT support Expo Managed Workflow**. If you want to use Appier SDK in Expo, eject to Bare Workflow.
> 
> 


* * *

## 

Dependencies Version Support

Supported Versions| Dependency  
---|---  
• **React** \- 16.0.0 and above   
• **React Native** \- 0.46.0 and above| [React Native WebView](https://github.com/react-native-community/react-native-webview) \- 5.0.1 and above  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> **For React Native versions 0.60.0 and above** ,
> 
>   * Use Appier SDK versions 1.3.1 and above only.
>   * Appier SDK versions 1.3.1 and above support CocoaPods installation and automatic linking.
> 

> 
> **For React Native versions 0.59.x and below** , Appier SDK version 1.3.1 is also supported.

* * *

## 

Release Notes

Release notes of Appier React Native SDK can be found [here](doc:react-native-sdk-release-notes).

* * *

## 

Release Candidates (Testing Only)

Any Appier SDK version with `rc` in the version number is a release candidate. When installing SDK, use release versions such as `1.3.0` or `1.3.1` instead of release candidates such as `1.3.1-rc-1` or `1.3.1-rc-2`. Release candidates may be unstable and are intended for testing purposes.

__Updated 11 months ago

# Can I also trigger a fake prompt for users who aren’t opted-in?

Yes. You can do this by calling `appier("prompt-push")` in the Appier web SDK.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> Unsubscribed users, or users who disabled web notifications on their device, wouldn’t get the fake prompt.

# Web Push for Safari

Use web push notifications to increase your marketing reach and user engagement, now supported for Safari 16.4+ (MacOS, iPadOS, iOS). For users who opt in, web push notifications can be sent from Progressive Web Apps (PWAs) that have been added to the device's home screen.

![](https://files.readme.io/ad94686-safari-web-push-example.png)

* * *

## 

User flow

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note: Safari permission prompt
> 
> Unlike other browsers, Safari doesn't allow apps to directly send opt-in prompts. Instead, users must first agree to the AIQUA opt-in prompt before the system's web push permission dialogue can be sent.

  1. The initial AIQUA web push opt-in prompt is displayed.
  2. If the user accepts the AIQUA opt-in prompt, the Safari system dialogue requesting permission to send web push notifications is automatically sent.
  3. After the user accepts **both** prompts (first the AIQUA opt-in prompt, then the Safari system dialogue), the user will become a web push subscriber and will be able to receive web push notifications.

![](https://files.readme.io/f6e7de7-web-push-notification-prompts.png)

* * *

## 

Setup steps

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Prerequisite
> 
> Your website must be [integrated with the Appier Web SDK](doc:integrating-with-the-aiqua-web-sdk#part-ii-integrating-the-sdk).

### 

1\. Create or update your web app's manifest file

Your website must be an installable [ Progressive Web App (PWA)](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Progressive_web_apps). To be an installable PWA, i.e. having the ability to be added to the home screen, your website must be served alongside a web app manifest file.

Add or update your web app's `manifest.json` file with the following required members:

Key| Description  
---|---  
`name`| The full name of your web app.  
`short_name`| An abbreviated name that's displayed when there isn't enough space to display the full name.  
`display`| The display mode that determines how much of the browser UI will be shown. For details on the possible values, refer to the [Mozilla Development Network reference](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Manifest/display).We recommend setting this to `standalone` for a native app-like experience.  
`theme_color`| The app's default theme color.  
`background_color`| The placeholder background color used for the splash screen when launching the app, before its stylesheet is loaded.  
`icons`| An array of icon image objects that can serve as app icons in different contexts. For more details about the `icons` objects, refer to the [Mozilla Development Network reference.](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Manifest/icons)  
`icons.src`| The path to the image file.  
`icons.sizes`| A string containing space-separated image dimensions.  
`icons.type`| The image's media type.  
  
A `manifest.json` with all the required fields looks like this:

JSON
    
    
    {
      "name": "<APP_NAME>",
      "short_name": "<SHORT_APP_NAME>",
      "display": "standalone",
      "theme_color": "<#000000>",
      "background_color": "<#000000>",
      "icons": [
        {
          "src": "/path/to/file/icon.png",
          "sizes": "256x256 512x512",
          "type": "img/png"
        }
      ]
    }
    

### 

2\. Link the manifest file to your web app

In your web app's HTML, include the manifest using a `<link>` element inside the `<head>` tag. Every page in your app must be linked to the manifest.

HTML
    
    
    <head>
      <link rel="manifest" href="manifest.json" />
    </head>
    

### 

3\. Set up the AIQUA opt-in prompt

Follow the instructions in [Setting Web Push Prompts (Web Pixel Settings)](doc:account-overview-web-pixel-settings#opt-in-prompt) to set up the initial AIQUA opt-in prompt.

The AIQUA opt-in prompt must be accepted by the user before the Safari system dialogue can be displayed, and the Safari system dialogue is automatically displayed after the AIQUA opt-in prompt is accepted. After accepting both prompts, the user will become a web push subscriber.

![](https://files.readme.io/7e8a35a-aiqua-web-push-opt-in-prompt.png)

### 

4\. Encourage users to add your website to their home screen (for iOS and iPadOS)

iOS and iPadOS users must manually add your website to their home screen before web push notifications can be sent. For example, you can send an [in-web campaign](doc:campaigns-in-web) guiding users browsing your website to add the website to their home screen.

### 

5\. Test a web push campaign

After you've completed the setup steps, test your web push campaign to verify that you can receive web push notifications on your device.

* * *

## 

Troubleshooting

If your users are unable to receive Safari web push notifications, please follow the troubleshooting steps below:

  1. Check your operating system version
  2. Ensure that your web app has been installed (iOS, iPadOS)
  3. Verify the user's web push subscription status
  4. Verify your Appier Web SDK integration



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Contact Appier Support
> 
> If you encounter difficulties during the troubleshooting process, please contact Appier Support ([[email protected]](/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection#1b7e686844686e6b6b74696f5b7a6b6b727e6935787476)) for assistance.

### 

1\. Check your operating system version

Safari web push is only supported for the following operating systems:

  * macOS Big Sur, Monterey, Ventura, or later
  * iPadOS 16.4 or later
  * iOS 16.4 or later



### 

2\. Ensure that your web app has been installed (iOS, iPadOS)

Ensure that the web app has been successfully added to the receiving device's home screen. To be installable, your PWA must include a web app manifest file containing all the required members.

### 

3\. Verify the user's web push subscription status

Verify whether the has successfully subscribed to web push notifications. To check the web push subscription status:

  1. Go to the AIQUA dashboard, click your account name in the bottom-left corner, then click **Recent Activity** and go to the **Web** tab.
  2. In the event log, check if the user completed a `subscribed_to_webpush` event. If you see this event, it means the user successfully subscribed to web push notifications.

![](https://files.readme.io/ed077c2-subscribed-to-webpush-event.png)

### 

4\. Verify your Appier Web SDK integration

Confirm that you've successfully integrated the Appier Web SDK by checking if the AIQUA service worker is installed and active on your website.

  1. Navigate to your website and accept both the AIQUA opt-in prompt and the Safari system dialogue requesting permission to send web push notifications.
  2. If you don't see the **Develop** menu in the menu bar, follow the instructions in this [Apple Support Guide](https://support.apple.com/en-om/guide/safari/sfri20948/mac) to add it.
  3. In the menu bar, click **Develop > Service Workers**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4e05227-safari-service-worker.png)

  3. Verify that your website's URL is listed under **Service Workers**. If you can find your website, it means that the AIQUA service worker has been properly installed.

# Web SDK Integration Overview

Integrating your website with Appier Web SDK is required if you want to:

  * [Log user data](doc:logging-data-on-the-web-sdk) for your website visitors
  * Send [Web Push notifications](doc:campaigns-getting-started-web-push)
  * Show [In-Web Campaigns](doc:campaigns-in-web) to users on your website
  * Generate and show a list of [recommended products](doc:recommendations-v2) on your website



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> Microsoft has [announced](https://blogs.windows.com/windowsexperience/2021/05/19/the-future-of-internet-explorer-on-windows-10-is-in-microsoft-edge/) that Windows 10 will no longer support Internet Explorer 11 after June 2022. Features provided by Appier Web SDK are not guaranteed to work for users who are using Internet Explorer.

## 

Integration Overview

There are four main parts to Web SDK integration. Parts III and IV are for sending Web Push notifications. To begin integration, proceed to [Integrating Appier Web SDK](doc:integrating-with-the-aiqua-web-sdk).

![1240](https://files.readme.io/5ec1743-Screen_Shot_2020-03-20_at_2.30.13_PM.png)

  * **Part I: Website Info** : On AIQUA dashboard, enable Web push notification and In-Web Campaigns.
  * **Part II: Integrate SDK** : Add `qg-service-worker.js` file and snippets of JavaScript code to your website.
  * **Part III: Send Notifications** : Send a test push notification to yourself.
  * **Part IV: Web Pixel Settings** : It is highly recommended to configure the permission prompts that ask users to subscribe to web push using [Web Pixel Settings](doc:account-overview-web-pixel-settings).



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> If your website is a Shopify store, refer to [Integrating Shopify Stores with Appier Web SDK](doc:integrating-shopify-stores-with-appier-web-sdk).

# Web SDK Integration Overview

Integrating your website with Appier Web SDK is required if you want to:

  * [Log user data](doc:logging-data-on-the-web-sdk) for your website visitors
  * Send [Web Push notifications](doc:campaigns-getting-started-web-push)
  * Show [In-Web Campaigns](doc:campaigns-in-web) to users on your website
  * Generate and show a list of [recommended products](doc:recommendations-v2) on your website



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> Microsoft has [announced](https://blogs.windows.com/windowsexperience/2021/05/19/the-future-of-internet-explorer-on-windows-10-is-in-microsoft-edge/) that Windows 10 will no longer support Internet Explorer 11 after June 2022. Features provided by Appier Web SDK are not guaranteed to work for users who are using Internet Explorer.

## 

Integration Overview

There are four main parts to Web SDK integration. Parts III and IV are for sending Web Push notifications. To begin integration, proceed to [Integrating Appier Web SDK](doc:integrating-with-the-aiqua-web-sdk).

![1240](https://files.readme.io/5ec1743-Screen_Shot_2020-03-20_at_2.30.13_PM.png)

  * **Part I: Website Info** : On AIQUA dashboard, enable Web push notification and In-Web Campaigns.
  * **Part II: Integrate SDK** : Add `qg-service-worker.js` file and snippets of JavaScript code to your website.
  * **Part III: Send Notifications** : Send a test push notification to yourself.
  * **Part IV: Web Pixel Settings** : It is highly recommended to configure the permission prompts that ask users to subscribe to web push using [Web Pixel Settings](doc:account-overview-web-pixel-settings).



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> If your website is a Shopify store, refer to [Integrating Shopify Stores with Appier Web SDK](doc:integrating-shopify-stores-with-appier-web-sdk).

# What's Quieter Permission UI?

Chrome introduced "Quieter Permission UI" for Web push in Chrome 80, where some users will no longer see the system prompts that ask for permission to send Web push notifications.

![](https://files.readme.io/03120ab-Screen_Shot_2020-03-20_at_1.49.27_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Who will experience Quieter Permission?

There are several situations where Quieter Permission will be applied to Chrome users:

  * **Manual Enrollment** \- Users can manually enroll in Quieter Permission by going to Chrome's Settings page > Privacy Security > Site Settings > Notifications, and then enabling "Use quieter messaging".
  * **Automatic Enrollment for Users Who Frequently Opt-Out** \- Chrome automatically enrolls users who frequently decline notification permission across various websites in Quieter Permission.
  * **Automatic Enrollment for Websites with Low Opt-in Rates** \- Chrome automatically enrolls websites with low opt-in rates in Quieter Permission.



* * *

## 

What are the impacts?

Users enrolled in Quieter Permission now need to manually click the notification icon in the browser's address bar if they want to subscribe to web push. This is expected to lower your opt-in rate.

On the upside, however, users who do choose to subscribe probably have higher intent and are more likely to engage.

![](https://files.readme.io/bc4dd6b-Screen_Shot_2020-04-10_at_1.39.48_PM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> Existing subscribers are not affected.

* * *

## 

What are AIQUA's solutions?

With the new Quieter Permission UI, it is now even more crucial for marketers to carefully consider user experience. Instead of indiscriminately showing system prompts to all users immediately upon site visit, AIQUA offers several features that help you ask for users' permission at the appropriate time with relevant messages.

The following feature can be configured in [In-Web Campaign](doc:campaigns-in-web-creative-subscription-boost):

  * **Subscription Boost** \- Subscription boost notifications are customizable messages that can pop up inside your website to encourage users to opt-in. Using triggering rules based on user's behavior, Subscription Boost can be shown at the contextually relevant moments in a user’s journey.



The following features can be configured in [Web Pixel Settings](doc:https://docs.aiqua.appier.com/docs/account-overview-web-pixel-settings):

  * **Opt-In Tip** \- A tip message to show users how to manually opt-in even if they do not see the browser's native system prompt.
  * **Opt-In Prompt (fake prompt)** \- This customizable opt-in prompt asks whether the users want to receive notification and only shows the actual system prompt if users agree. This lowers the chance of users declining the system prompt and the chance of your website being considered as having "low opt-in rate" by Chrome.
  * **Delay the timing of the system prompts** \- You may want to delay the time to show system prompt and space out the time between two system prompts.



The following feature can be configured by contacting [Appier Support](/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection#6a0f191935191f1a1a05181e2a0b1a1a030f1844090507):

  * **Custom timing for system prompt** \- Instead of showing system prompt upon users' site visit, you can set the system prompt to only pop up after specific conditions (e.g. after the user completed a purchase). You need to contact Appier Support to enable this feature, and then use `appier("prompt-push")` to call the function in Appier web SDK.

# Why do my app push campaigns have low delivery rates?

## 

Overview

This FAQ describes how AIQUA delivers mobile app push notifications, discusses several reasons which may cause undelivered app push notifications, and provides a solution to mitigate low delivery rates for app push campaigns:

  * Factors that can affect push notification delivery rates
  * Factors that don't affect push notification delivery rates
  * Improving delivery rate: Segmenting by active users



### 

Push notification delivery rate

The delivery rate for push campaigns can be calculated using `Impressions / Total Sent`, where:

  * `Total sent`: The total number of push notifications sent
  * `Impressions`: The total number of push notifications received on mobile devices, determined by the number of `notification_received` events which are automatically logged by the Appier SDK upon receiving a push notification



> 👍
> 
> Both the `Total sent` and `Impressions` metrics are visible from the [campaign list page](doc:campaigns-regular-manage#search-and-filter-campaigns).

### 

Understanding AIQUA's push notification mechanism

**Push campaign audience** : When an AIQUA app push campaign is run, a push notification is sent to Android or iOS subscribers in the campaign audience, depending on which platform the campaign is targeting. Users satisfying the following conditions are considered subscribers:

  * The app is installed on the user's device
  * The user has opted in to your app's push notifications (`aiq_push_enabled`)
  * The user has a valid push token (`gcmId`)



**Push notification delivery** : AIQUA delivers push notifications using [Firebase Cloud Messaging (FCM)](https://firebase.google.com/docs/cloud-messaging) or Apple Push Notification service (APNs). After the notification is delivered by AIQUA to the notification service, AIQUA only knows if the notification has been successfully delivered if the Appier SDK logs a push impression event (`notification_received`). The SDK automatically logs a `notification_received` event for each push notification that is received.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Delivery issues occurring at the notification service level, i.e. due to FCM or APNs, can't be prevented by AIQUA.

**Push notification priority level** : To ensure that notifications can be received consistently across platforms and device state (e.g. when your app is killed or running in the background), AIQUA sends notifications to the notification service (FCM or APNs) with the following priority levels:

  * [FCM message priority](https://firebase.google.com/docs/cloud-messaging/android/message-priority): High priority
  * [APNs notification priority (`apns-priority`)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/usernotifications/setting_up_a_remote_notification_server/sending_notification_requests_to_apns#2947607): 10



* * *

## 

Factors that can affect push notification delivery rates

The following factors can affect your campaign's push delivery rate:

  * Blackout windows (account settings)
  * Time to live expired (campaign settings)
  * Delay in uninstalled app token deletion
  * FCM message overwrite (Android)
  * Background service restrictions (Android)
  * Battery optimization



### 

Blackout windows (account settings)

If you've set a [blackout window](doc:user-settings#blackout-window) in your account settings to avoid disturbing users or to comply with local regulations, your campaign notifications may go undelivered. When a blackout window is active:

  * AIQUA strictly avoids sending any notifications to users
  * If the notification's time to live (TTL) has expired, the notification won't be delivered



### 

Time to live expired (campaign settings)

If your campaign has a notification time to live (TTL) configured, and the device can't establish a network connection within the specified TTL, the notification will expire and go undelivered.

Notification services, such as FCM or Apple Push Notification service, are unable to deliver push notifications to users who aren't connected to the internet. Notifications are only sent after the connection is re-established, which can result in delayed delivery or TTL-expired (undelivered) messages.

![](https://files.readme.io/9e20942-ttl.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Although a short TTL may reduce delivery rates to offline devices, notifications with a TTL of 0 seconds are sent by notification services (FCM, APNs) without throttling, allowing for the lowest notification delivery latency.

### 

Delay in uninstalled app token deletion

There is a delay between the time a user uninstalls an app and when the app registration token is removed by notification service, i.e. FCM/APNs.

During this interim period, the notification service will still recognize the app as installed on that user's device (due to the registration token still being present) and will give these notifications a valid message ID even though it's undeliverable, as the user has already uninstalled the app. Since the app has already been uninstalled and the user has no way of receiving the notification, the notification will go undelivered.

### 

FCM message overwrite (Android)

AIQUA delivers notifications to offline devices using ["collapsible" messages](https://firebase.google.com/docs/cloud-messaging/concept-options#collapsible_and_non-collapsible_messages) with a single collapse key. When FCM receives more than one "collapsible" message destined for a single device, only the latest message is queued, causing all previous messages to be deleted. This means that messages that haven't yet been delivered by FCM will be deleted if a new message is add to the queue.

For example, if FCM receives two messages from AIQUA, one after the other, destined for a single device, and the device is offline (hence unable to receive notifications), the device will only receive the latest message upon reconnecting to the network.

  1. The first message is queued by FCM, but can't be delivered since the destination device is offline
  2. The second message is queued, but since messages share a single collapse key, the first message is deleted
  3. When the device reconnects to the network, only the latest message is delivered



### 

Background service restrictions (Android)

Apps that the Android operating system determines are not frequently used may be placed in the "Rare" priority bucket. The operating system imposes strict limitations to apps in this priority bucket—they're subject to certain restrictions (e.g. running jobs, triggering alarms) in addition to [power management restrictions](https://developer.android.com/topic/performance/power/power-details), which reduce the apps ability to connect to the internet.

> 👍
> 
> To learn more about Android's priority buckets, see [App Standby Buckets](https://developer.android.com/topic/performance/appstandby#restricted-bucket).

### 

Battery optimization

When battery optimization features are enabled on a device, background processes can be stopped or restricted, which may limit device connectivity with notification services and potentially impacting push notification delivery.

* * *

## 

Factors that don't affect push notification delivery rates

  * "Do Not Disturb" mode
  * Notification frequency cap (account settings)



### 

"Do Not Disturb" mode

When "Do Not Disturb" ([Android](https://support.google.com/android/answer/9069335?hl=en), [iOS](https://support.apple.com/en-us/HT204321)) is enabled on a device, all notifications are hidden from the user's view. Although users don't see notifications when "Do Not Disturb" mode is enabled, notifications are still delivered in the background, and delivery rate is unaffected.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Although your campaign's delivery rate may not be affected by devices with "Do Not Disturb" enabled, conversion rates may decrease if fewer users see and interact with the notifications that have been delivered.

### 

Notification frequency cap (account settings)

[Setting a notification frequency cap](doc:user-settings#notification-frequency-cap) in your account settings prevents campaign notifications from being sent if your account has exceeded the cap. Although this setting prevents the sending of notifications exceeding the frequency cap, it has no effect on your campaign's delivery rate since AIQUA doesn't send the notifications in the first place.

* * *

## 

Improving delivery rate: Segmenting by active users

To reach users more effectively, you can segment by active app users (e.g. users who launched the app in the past seven days). Active app users are more likely to have a higher delivery rate, since your app is less likely to be subject to background service restrictions by the device operating system if your app is frequently used. Reserve app push campaigns for these users.

For the inactive users segment, you can try to use other channels to motivate them to launch your app in order to prevent your app from becoming labeled as "inactive" by the device operating system.

# Add Emails to the Unsubscribed Email List

Ask AI

This POST request adds the specified emails to the unsubscribed list.

This API supports idempotency which allows you to make multiple requests for the same email address without causing conflicts or issues.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| POST  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token <your api token>", "appid": "<your appid>"}  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/unsubscribe>  
Body| {"emails":["[user01@example.com](mailto:user01@example.com)",...]}  
Success Response| 

  * 200 OK {"status": "update in progress"}

  
Error Codes| 

  * 400 Bad Request {"error": "Invalid JSON request"}
  * 400 Bad Request {"error": "Invalid "emails" parameter"}

  
  
#### 

Sample Request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X POST \
         -H 'appid: <your app_id>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <your token>' \
         -d '{"emails": ["user01@example.com",...]}' \
         'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/unsubscribe'

  * Request body cannot contain more than 50000 email addresses and must be under 1 MB.
  * A `{"status": "update in progress"}` response indicates that the request is being processed.
  * Later, you can [retrieve the unsubscribed email list](/reference/retrieve-unsubscribed-emails) to see if the emails are added to the list, or retrieve the user profiles using [this api](/reference/retrieve-users) to make sure the user's `email_unsubscribe` field is now set to `true`.

# Getting Started

Ask AI

## 

Overview

The AIQUA API endpoints provide programmatic access to several platform features, such as report exports, and Recommendation, and offline data uploading.

* * *

## 

Authentication

When making requests to many of the AIQUA API endpoints, you'll need to supply your account's app ID and API token in the request header.

The following sample cURL request demonstrates what your header should look like:

JSON
    
    
    curl -X HTTP-METHOD 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3' \
        -H "Authorization: Token &lt;API_TOKEN&gt;" \
        -H "appid: &lt;APP_ID&gt;"

### 

Retrieving your app ID and API key

  1. Click your account name in the lower-left corner of the screen.
  2. Select **Account Settings**. From the settings page, you'll be able to find your **App ID** and **API Token**.

![](https://files.readme.io/885cd24-app-id-api-token.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Depending on the access rights of your account, the API token may not be visible. If you can't view the API token, contact your account administrator.
> 
> For details about account permissions, see [Access Control List (ACL)](/docs/user-settings#access-control-list-acl).

# Retrieve the Status of an Offline User Upload Job

Ask AI

get

https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/jobs/{job_id}

Retrieve the status of a single user upload job by job ID.

Path Params

job_id

string

required

The job ID returned in the response after [creating an offline user upload job](/reference/create-an-offline-user-upload-job).

Query Params

errors

int32

Set to `1` to return a debug response containing error details.

Headers

appid

string

required

Required. Your AIQUA app ID.

authorization

string

required

Required. Your AIQUA API token. Must start with `"Token"`.

Responses

# 

`` 200

Success

# 

`` 404

Failure

# Retrieve the Status of Multiple Offline User Upload Jobs

Ask AI

Retrieve the status of multiple offline user upload jobs by group ID or job creation date range.

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
**`GET`**| ` https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/jobs`  
  
## 

Headers

Name| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
`appid`| Required. Your AIQUA app ID.| `abc123`  
`authorization`| Required. Your AIQUA API token. Must start with `"Token"`.| `Token a1bc3d4e5`  
  
## 

Query parameters

There are two ways to retrieve the status of multiple offline user upload jobs:

  * Retrieving status by date range
  * Retrieving status by group ID



### 

Retrieving status by date range

Return the status of all jobs created in the specified period. Note that upload jobs expire after two months.

Name| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
`date_from`| The beginning of the date range to return results for.| `"20240101"`  
`date_to`| The end of the date range to return results for.| `"20240102"`  
  
### 

Retrieving status by group ID

Name| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
`group_id`| Return the status of all jobs associated with the specified group ID.| `"group_123"`  
  
* * *

## 

Sample requests

By Group IDBy Date Range
    
    
    curl -H 'appid: <appid>' \
      	 -H 'authorization: Token <token>' \
      	 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/jobs?group_id=group_123'
    
    
    curl -H 'appid: <appid>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <token>' \
      	 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/jobs?date_from=20201001&date_to=20201031'

## 

Sample responses

By Group IDBy Date Range
    
    
    {
        "matched_jobs": {
            "total": 4,
            "queued": 0,
            "finished": 3,
            "in_progress": 0,
            "fatal_error": 0,
            "invalid_schema": 1
        },
        "received_lines": 1000,
        "aggregation": {
            "created": 76,
            "updated": 980,
            "invalid": 5,
            "error": 0      
        },
        "jobs_with_invalid_records": ["1578458079-356066", "1578458079-356066"],
        "group_id": "group_123"
    }
    
    
    {
        "matched_jobs": {
            "total": 4,
            "queued": 0,
            "finished": 3,
            "in_progress": 0,
            "fatal_error": 0,
            "invalid_schema": 1
        },
        "received_lines": 1000,
        "aggregation": {
            "created": 76,
            "updated": 980,
            "invalid": 5,
            "error": 0      
        },
        "jobs_with_invalid_records": ["1578458079-356066", "1578458079-356066"],
        "date_from": "20201001",
        "date_to": "20201031"
    }

Under `matched_jobs`, you can see the upload results by the number of upload jobs.

  * `finished`: The number of upload jobs where at least one user profile is uploaded successfully. 
    * Upload jobs that are partially completed are still counted as `finished`. The lines of user profiles that failed to upload are counted under `invalid` if due to invalid schema, or under `error` if due to server errors.
  * `fatal_error`: The number of upload jobs where all user profiles failed to upload due to server errors. These lines are counted as `error`.
  * `invalid_schema`: The number of upload jobs that do not contain any valid lines. These invalid lines are counted as `invalid`.



Under `aggregation`, you can see the upload results by the number of user profiles.

  * `created`: The number of unmatched offline users created in the common database.
  * `updated`: The number of user profiles merged.
  * `invalid`: The number of invalid user profiles. (e.g. incorrect data format, missing identifiers...etc)
  * `error`: The number of user profiles that encountered server errors.



To retrieve error details, make a request to the `/jobs` endpoints with one of the job IDs listed in `jobs_with_invalid_records` specified in the `job_id` path parameter and the `errors=1` query parameter. For example: `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/jobs/{job_id}/?errors=1`.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The `updated` count may exceed the `received_lines` because a single offline user profile may be merged multiple times. For example, once with an Android user profile, and once with an iOS user profile.

* * *

## 

Status codes

Code| Status| Description  
---|---|---  
**`200`**| ` OK`| Status successfully retrieved.  
**`400`**| ` Bad Request`| Invalid date range or group ID.

# Create a Campaign

Ask AI

In AIQUA, there are two ways for you to set up campaign: by using the [AIQUA Dashboard](https://aiqua.appier.com/), or by using the API.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Only legacy regular push campaigns for a single channel can be created via API.
> 
> To create merged push campaigns (multi-channel), [create a campaign on the AIQUA dashboard](/docs/campaigns-regular-create-push) instead.

Follow the steps below to set up regular campaigns for push notifications using the AIQUA API:

## 

1\. Create JSON Request Files

Refer to these [schema files](https://docs.google.com/uc?export=download&id=1vQum7gXTLB69p3Ns_nzazLWkcVZEfexD) to create the JSON request files. Inside the zip file, you will find the root schema file `Campaign.schema.json`, which includes schemas for different types of campaign creative and platform.

Here's a sample request:

JSON
    
    
    {
        "type": "regular",
        "channel": "push",
        "platform": "web",
        "name": "abc regular campaign",
        "include_segments": [
            18388
        ],
        "exclude_segments": [
            21781
        ],
        "creative" : {
            "ttl": 86400,
            "notification": {
                "type": "web",
                "title": "ABC Friday Sales",
                "body":  "Special offers for online purchases every Friday",
                "icon": "https://imgurl01.com/logoABC.png",
                "destination": "https://www.abc.com/friday-offer",
                "image": "https://imgurl01.com/abc.jpg",
                "image_type": "standard"
            }
        }
    
    }

## 

2\. Make the Request

Make the request using the files you created in step 1:

JSON
    
    
    curl -H 'appid: <appid>' -H 'authorization: Token <token>' \
         -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d @request.json \
         https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/campaigns/

You should see a response similar to this:

JSON
    
    
    {"created": "2019-11-18 07:04:24", "id": 200208, "name": "abc regular campaign"}

# Create a Report Job

Ask AI

## 

Endpoint URL

HTTP method| Endpoint URL  
---|---  
**`POST`**| ` https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/`  
  
## 

Authorization and headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token: <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
* * *

## 

Request

### 

Body parameters

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`report_type`| string| Required. The type of report job to create. Supported options:• `"campaign.details"`: Create a job to export a campaign performance report• `"campaign.interaction.data"`: Create a job to export a campaign user report• `"seg.file.generator"`: Create a job to export a segment report  
`parameters`| `Parameters`| Required. An object containing report job settings. The `Parameters` object will differ depending on what type of report you're creating this job for, i.e. the value of `report_type`:  
  
• `Parameters` object for campaign performance reports  
• `Parameters` object for campaign user reports  
• `Parameters` object for segment reports  
  
#### 

`Parameters` object for campaign performance reports

Include the following fields in the `parameters` object when creating a job for a campaign performance report (`"report_type": "campaign.details"`).

You can specify which campaigns to include in the campaign performance report (`"report_type": "campaign.details"`) by specifying one of:

  1. `parameters.campaign_ids`: A list of up to 5000 campaign IDs
  2. `parameters.date`: A date range from which campaigns have run in; the report will contain data for all campaigns that ran in the specified date range 
     * The start and end date must be within 180 days of the current date
     * The full date range must be less than or equal to 31 days (end date - start date ≤ 31 days)
     * Only campaigns that have run in the past 180 days will be included in the report; however, if a campaign is included in the report, all of its performance data will be contained as well, including data beyond the past 180 days



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Important
> 
> In the `parameters` object, include only one of `campaign_ids` or `date` to determine which campaigns to include in the report—don't include both.

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`campaign_type`| string| Required. Only specify one of the following supported values: • `"regular"` • `"trigger"` • `"inweb"` • `"inapp"`  
`campaign_ids`| list| Only required if `parameters.dates` is omitted. A list of IDs of the campaigns to include in the report.Campaign performance data corresponding to any campaign IDs which don't belong to the specified `campaign_type` will be omitted from the report.  
`dates.start`| string| Only required if `campaign_ids` is omitted. The start of the report period, with the format `YYYY-MM-DD`.  
`dates.end`| string| Only required if `campaign_ids` is omitted. The end of the report period, with the format `YYYY-MM-DD`.  
  
#### 

`Parameters` object for campaign user reports

Include the following fields in the `parameters` object when creating a job for a campaign user report (`"report_type": "campaign.interaction.data"`).

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`date.start`| string| Required. The start of the report period, with the format `YYYY-MM-DD`. Up to 60 days before the current date.  
`date.end`| string| Required. The end of the report period, with the format`YYYY-MM-DD`.  
`campaign_id`| integer| Required. The ID of the campaign you're generating the report for.  
`attribution_type`| string| Required. The [attribution model](/docs/performance-and-reports-understanding-event-attribution#understanding-attribution-models) used to calculate conversions. Available options:• `"overall"`: Corresponds to the **Last-View & Last-Click** attribution model • `"last_clicked"`: Corresponds to the **Last-Click** attribution model • `"last_viewed"`: Corresponds to the **Last-View** attribution model  
  
#### 

`Parameters` object for segment reports

Include the following field in the `parameters` object when creating a job for a segment report (`"report_type": "seg.file.generator"`).

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`segment_id`| integer| The ID of the segment you're generating the report for.  
  
### 

Example request: Campaign performance report

Request: report by campaign IDRequest: report by date range
    
    
    curl --location --request POST 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --header 'Authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
    --header 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
    --data-raw '{
        "report_type": "campaign.details",
        "parameters": {
            "campaign_type": "regular",
            "campaign_ids": [123, 456, 789]
        }
    }'
    
    
    curl --location --request POST 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --header 'Authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
    --header 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
    --data-raw '{
        "report_type": "campaign.details",
        "parameters": {
            "campaign_type": "regular",
            "dates": {
                "start": "2020-04-15",
                "end": "2020-04-30"
            }
        }
    }'

### 

Example request: Campaign user report

cURL
    
    
    curl --location --request POST 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --header 'Authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
    --header 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
    --data-raw '{
      "report_type": "campaign.interaction.data",
      "parameters": {
        "dates": {
          "start": "2023-03-01",
          "end": "2023-03-06"
        },
        "campaign_id": 123,
        "attribution_type": "overall"
      }
    }'

### 

Example request: Segment report

cURL
    
    
    curl --location --request POST 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --header 'Authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
    --header 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
    --data-raw '{
      "report_type": "seg.file.generator",
      "parameters": {
        "segment_id": 123
      }
    }'

* * *

## 

Response

Use the job ID (`id`) contained in the response to [retrieve the status of the report job](/reference/retrieve-the-status-of-a-report-job).

### 

Example response: Campaign performance report

Response: report by campaign IDResponse: report by date range
    
    
    {
        "report_type": "campaign.details",
        "parameters": {
            "campaign_type": "regular",
            "campaign_ids": [
                123,
                456,
                789
            ],
            "format": "csv",
            "app_id": "<APP_ID>"
        },
        "id": "<JOB_ID>"
    }
    
    
    {
        "report_type": "campaign.details",
        "parameters": {
            "campaign_type": "regular",
            "dates": {
                "start": "2021-03-01",
                "end": "2021-03-30"
            },
            "format": "csv",
            "app_id": "<APP_ID>"
        },
        "id": "<JOB_ID>"
    }

### 

Example response: Campaign user report

JSON
    
    
    {
        "report_type": "campaign.interaction.data",
        "parameters": {
            "dates": {
                "end": "2023-03-01",
                "start": "2023-03-06"
            },
            "format": "csv",
            "campaign_id": 123,
            "attribution_type": "overall",
            "app_id": "<APP_ID>"
        },
        "id": "<JOB_ID>"
    }

### 

Example response: Segment report

JSON
    
    
    {
       "report_type": "seg.file.generator",
       "parameters": {
           "segment_ids": [
               123
           ],
           "app_id": "<APP_ID>"
       },
       "id": "<JOB_ID>"
    }

* * *

## 

Error codes

Code| Message| Description  
---|---|---  
`400`| `"Segment size too big"`| The segment you're attempting to export exceeds that maximum supported export size (1.2 million users).  
`400`| `"Missing {parameter}"`| `parameter` is missing from the request body.  
`400`| `"Invalid Segment ID: {segment_id}"`| `segment_id` is invalid.  
`400`| `"Reports for All Users Segment is forbidden"`| The request is attempting to create an export job for the **All Users** segment. Exporting reports for the **All Users** segment is unsupported.  
`400`| `"Cannot export segment report when segment is still being calculated"`| The target segment is still being calculated, and report exports are unavailable.  
`400`| `"Cannot export segment report for segment group"`| The request is attempting to create an export job for a [split segment](/docs/campaigns-getting-started-email-ip-warmup#split-users-into-multiple-segments). Exporting reports for split segments is unsupported.  
`400`| `"Cannot export segment report for offline event segment"`| The request is attempting to create an export job for an offline segment. Exporting reports for offline segments is unsupported.

# Create a Segment by Mapping with User List

Ask AI

In AIQUA, you can create a segment based on an uploaded file. For example, if you have a list of emails from customers who attended an event, you can upload this email list to create a segment. Emails that do not match with any existing user in AIQUA database will not be included in the segment.

There are two ways to do this: [by using the AIQUA dashboard](/docs/segments-create-mapping-with-user-list), or by using the API. Refer to the following steps to upload your segment using the AIQUA API.

## 

1\. Upload the Segment File

The upload file must meet the following requirements:

  * The supported encoding is UTF-8 without BOM.
  * The file must have one value on each line without any spaces, tabs, or commas.
  * The maximum file size is 100 MB.



Sample Request:

cURL
    
    
    curl -H 'Authorization: Token <your token>'\
         -H 'appId: <your app id>' \
         -H 'content-type: multipart/form-data'\
         -F file=@/path/to/your/file\
         https://aiqua.appier.com/qganalyzedata/upload-segment-file/

Sample Response:

Shell
    
    
    {"filename": "upload.csv1495733409.41"}

Here, `upload.csv1495733409.41` is the temporary name of the file that has been created on the server. You'll need this name in the second step.

## 

2\. Create the Segment Using the Temporary File

Use the temporary filename returned in the response to create a segment.

cURL
    
    
    curl -X POST\
         -H 'Authorization: Token <your token>'\
         -H 'appId: <your app id>' \
         -H 'content-type: application/json'\
         -d '{"name": "<name of the segment>", "description": "<description of the segment>", "filename": "<filename produced in step 1>", "field": "<field name whose values are present in the file>"}'\
         https://aiqua.appier.com/qganalyzedata/upload_segment/

Sample Request: Below is a sample request with `field` set to `email` to indicate that the uploaded file contains email addresses.

cURL
    
    
    curl -X POST\
         -H 'Authorization: Token <your token>'\
         -H 'appId: <your app id>'
         -H 'content-type: application/json'\
         -d '{"name": "my uploaded segment", "description": "This is a bunch of emails", "filename": "upload.csv1495733409.41", "field": "email"}'\
         https://aiqua.appier.com/qganalyzedata/upload_segment/

A segment will be created from this request and a `segmentId` would be returned.

# Create an Offline User Upload Job

Ask AI

Create a job to upload offline users.

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
**`POST`**| ` https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/`  
  
## 

Rate limits

Please note the following rate limits when making requests to this endpoint using the same app ID:

  * 2 requests per second
  * 20 requests per minute
  * 250 requests per day



If the rate limit is exceeded, this endpoint returns a **`429 Too Many Requests`** response.

## 

Headers

Name| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
`appid`| Required. Your AIQUA app ID.| `abc123`  
`authorization`| Required. Your AIQUA API token. Must start with `"Token"`.| `Token a1bc3d4e5`  
`X_BULK_UPLOAD_GROUP_ID`| Optional. Your custom group ID. Must not exceed 16 characters and can only include characters, numbers, and the following special characters: `-`, `_`, `.`.| `group_0001`  
  
## 

Parameters

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`profiles`| File| The offline user data file to upload. To ensure a successful upload, ensure the uploaded file meets all the requirements.  
  
## 

Offline user file requirements

Ensure your offline user data file meets the following requirements before uploading it. Invalid data in the input file isn't uploaded. If an input file contains both invalid and valid data, the valid data will still upload successfully.

  * The data file must be a UTF-8 text (`txt`) file containing [NDJSON](https://github.com/ndjson/ndjson-spec) data not exceeding 100 MB. Other encodings, including UTF-8 BOM, are not supported.
  * User attribute names must follow the guidelines in [Field Names for Custom Data](/docs/custom-user-data#field-names-for-custom-data).
  * Each offline user profile in the data file must be a JSON object containing at least one of the following unique identifiers as a string: 
    * `user_id`
    * `phoneNo`
    * `email`
    * `line_uid`
  * Offline user profiles with duplicate unique identifiers are considered invalid and will not be uploaded.
  * Don't include the following fields in the data file: 
    * `gcmId`
    * `userId`
    * `_id`
    * `mTime`



A valid input data file containing four users looks like this:

offline_users.txt
    
    
    { "user_id": "5ab75f14b28ce1740b492e75", "custom_key": 20, "city": "Taipei" }
    { "phoneNo": "0123456789", "custom_key": 10, "city": "Tainan" }
    { "email": "user@example.com", "custom_key": 30, "city": "Taichung" }
    { "line_uid": "U5ab75f14b28ce1740b492e75", "custom_key": 20, "city": "Kaohsiung" }

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> User profiles are processed in batches of 10,000 records.

* * *

## 

Sample request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X POST \
         -H 'appid: 00001' \
         -H 'authorization: Token 12345' \
         -H 'X_BULK_UPLOAD_GROUP_ID: group_123' \
         https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/ -F profiles=@/path/to/your/file

## 

Sample response

JSON
    
    
    {
        "status": "queued",
        "job_id": "1603784671-722473",
        "message": "check status using job_id"
    }

Use `job_id` to [query the status of the offline user upload job](/reference/bulk-upload-offline-users-retrieve-the-status-of-offline-user-upload-jobs).

* * *

## 

Status codes

Code| Status| Description  
---|---|---  
`200`| `OK`| • The job was successfully submitted.  
`400`| `Bad Request`| • Invalid data file.  
• Data file without UTF-8 encoded is invalid.  
• X_BULK_UPLOAD_GROUP_ID is invalid.  
`500`| `Internal Server Error`| • failed to save the request file  
• could not process, please try again

# Create an Offline Event Segment (v1)

Ask AI

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Deprecation notice
> 
> The Offline Event API v1 is deprecated and is only supported for legacy users. Please use the [Offline Event API v2](/reference/getting-starting-with-the-offline-event-api-v2) to upload offline event data instead.

> 📘
> 
> Offline event segments don't contain event data by default. After creating an offline event segment, [upload offline event data](/reference/upload-offline-event-data) to the segment.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| **`POST`**  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token `<your api token>`", "appid": "`<your appid>`"}  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/>  
Success Response Code| **`200 - OK`**  
Error Codes| **`400 - Bad Request`**  
  
### 

Sample Request

Use a unique name for each offline-event segment.

cURL
    
    
    curl -d '{"name": "my-offline-event-segment"}'  \
         -H 'appid: <your_app_id>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <your_api_token>' \
         https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/

### 

Sample Response

JSON
    
    
    {
      "id":28839,
      "name":"my-offline-event-segment",
      "created":"2020-07-20T05:49:12.520535Z"
    }

`id`, the offline event segment's ID, is required when [uploading offline event data](/reference/upload-offline-event-data) to the segment.

# Create an Offline Segment Source (v2)

Ask AI

Create an offline segment source, which is the data source used to create an offline event segment.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> You can create up to three offline segment sources.

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
`POST`| `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events/segment`  
  
## 

Authorization and headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
* * *

## 

Body parameters

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`name`| string| The name of the offline segment source.  
  
* * *

## 

Example request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X 'POST' \
      'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events/segment' \
      -H 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
      -H 'authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
      -H 'accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      -d '{
      "name": "offline_segment_name"
    }'

## 

Example response

200 OK
    
    
    {
    	"metadata": {
    		"code": "success",
    		"reason": "",
    		"requestId": "FK7ZgfPToC4R3v7JKu7B2g"
    	},
    	"data": {
    		"id": 321334,
    		"name": "offline_segment_name",
    		"created": "2023-05-12T05:57:06.723264Z"
    	}
    }

# Delete an Offline Event Segment (v1)

Ask AI

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Deprecation notice
> 
> The Offline Event API v1 is deprecated and is only supported for legacy users. Please use the [Offline Event API v2](/reference/getting-starting-with-the-offline-event-api-v2) to upload offline event data instead.

The Delete Offline Event Segment API can be used to delete both offline-event source and derived segments. To delete an offline-event source segment, any offline-event derived segments under it must be deleted first.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| DELETE  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token `<your api token>`", "appid": "`<your appid>`"}  
URL| [https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/{segment_id}/](https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/%7Bsegment_id%7D/)  
Success Response Code| 204 - No Content  
Error Codes| 400 - Bad request  
404 - Not found  
  
### 

Sample Request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X DELETE \
        -H 'appid: <your_app_id>'  \
        -H 'authorization: Token <your_api_token>' \
        https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/<segment_id>/

# Delete Users

Ask AI

The Delete Users API allows you to delete user data for up to 10,000 users per request, e.g. for GDPR compliance, by providing a list of user identifiers. Deleted data can't be recovered.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note: Cached data
> 
> Deleted user data may still be accessible for a few days due to caching. Users whose data has been deleted may still receive campaigns in the form of push notifications, SMS, and emails for up to three days until the cache is cleared.
> 
> To ensure mobile users don't receive additional campaigns before the cache has cleared, regenerate the user ID using the Appier SDK methods:
> 
>   * [Regenerating iOS users](/docs/regenerate-user-id-ios)
>   * [Regenerating Android users](/docs/managing-android-users)
> 


## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| Endpoint URL  
---|---  
**`DELETE`**| ` api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/`  
  
## 

Headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token: <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
* * *

## 

User file

The user file is a JSON file that contains the list of user identifiers. Specify the path of the user file in the `users` form field in the request. The user file has the following requirements:

  * The user file must consist of an object with a single key, where the key is one of the following identifiers. 
    * `user_id`
    * `phoneNo`
    * `email`
    * `IDFA`
    * `AAID`
  * The key's value should consist of a list of identifier values specifying which users to delete.
  * Use lowercase letters in email addresses.
  * The user file must not exceed 1 MB.
  * The user file can't contain more than 10,000 user identifiers.



For example, the following example file uses `user_id` as an identifier to specify which users to delete.

example_users_file.json
    
    
    {
        "user_id": ["a735b29c26dc", "f23d0ab09245e", "8160ad0b42c4", "d3c64aadff98"]
    }

* * *

## 

Example request

Pass the JSON file into the `users` form field when you make the request. All data will be deleted for users who match the identifier(s) listed in the `users` file.

In the following example, users with IDs matching those specified in the `example_users_file.json` will be deleted.

cURLPython
    
    
    curl --location --request DELETE 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/' \
    --header 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
    --header 'Authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
    --form 'users=@"example_users_file.json"'
    
    
    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.qgraph.io/api/v3/users/"
    
    payload={}
    files=[
      ('users',('example_users_file.json',open('/path/to/your/file/example_users_file.json','rb'),'application/json'))
    ]
    headers = {
      'appid': '<YOUR_APP_ID>',
      'Authorization': 'Token <YOUR_API_TOKEN>'
    }
    
    response = requests.request("DELETE", url, headers=headers, data=payload, files=files)
    
    print(response.text)

## 

Example response

200 OK
    
    
    {
        "deleted":3,
        "objects":{
          "user_id": ["a735b29c26dc", "8160ad0b42c4", "d3c64aadff98"]
        }                                   
    }

## 

HTTP response codes

Code| Description  
---|---  
**`200 OK`**|  See the example response.  
**`400 Bad Request`**|  • `"Invalid JSON data"`  
• `"Should have exactly one identifier"`  
• `"Invalid identifier <key>"`  
• `"<key> is not an array"`  
• `"The email criteria exceeds the limitation of 10000"`

# Retrieve the Offline Event Schema (v2)

Ask AI

Retrieve your offline event schema.

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
`GET`| `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events`  
  
## 

Authorization and headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
* * *

## 

Example request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X 'GET' \
      'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events' \
      -H 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
      -H 'authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
      -H 'accept: application/json'

## 

Example response

200 OK
    
    
    {
        "metadata": {
            "code": "success",
            "reason": "",
            "requestId": "Mpf6mW5449gfLhEkeUKDs5249"
        },
        "data": {
            "parameters": [
                {
                    "name": "product_name",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": true
                },
                {
                    "name": "product_id",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": true
                },
                {
                    "name": "product_price",
                    "dataType": "float",
                    "required": true
                },
                {
                    "name": "order_id",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "category",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "product_brand",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "purchase_quantity",
                    "dataType": "integer",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "purchase_date",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "product_cycle",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "product_color",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "offline_purchased",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "store_no",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                },
                {
                    "name": "multicolor",
                    "dataType": "string",
                    "required": false
                }
            ]
        }
    }

# Retrieve the Offline Segment Source List (v2)

Ask AI

Retrieve a list of all your offline user segment sources.

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
`GET`| `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events/segment`  
  
## 

Authorization and headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
* * *

## 

Example request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X 'GET' \
      'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events/segment' \
      -H 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
      -H 'authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
      -H 'accept: application/json'

## 

Example response

200 OK
    
    
    {
      "metadata": {
        "code": "success",
        "reason": "",
        "requestId": “hxdTTrEmH2NPL5poeKsKnj"
      },
      "data": [
        {
          "id": 321334,
          "type": "offline_event_source",
          "name": "offline_segment_name",
          "description": "Offline event segment source created via API",
          "period": {
            "to": 0,
            "from": 0
          },
          "created": "2023-05-12T05:57:07Z",
          "modified": "2023-05-12T05:57:07Z"
        }
      ]
    }

# Remove Emails from the Unsubscribed Email List

Ask AI

This POST request removes the specified emails from the unsubscribed list. Once removed, these users will be eligible to receive email campaigns again.

This API supports idempotency which allows you to make multiple requests for the same email address without causing conflicts or issues.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| POST  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token <your api token>", "appid": "<your appid>"}  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/subscribe>  
Body| {"emails":["[user01@example.com](mailto:user01@example.com)",...]}  
Success Response| 

  * 200 OK {"status": "update in progress"}

  
Error Codes| 

  * 400 Bad Request {"error": "Invalid JSON request"}
  * 400 Bad Request {"error": "Invalid "emails" parameter"}

  
  
### 

Sample Request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X POST \
         -H 'appid: <your app_id>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <your token>' \
         -d '{"emails": ["user01@example.com",...]}' \
         'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/subscribe'

  * Request body cannot contain more than 50000 email addresses and must be under 1 MB.
  * A `{"status": "update in progress"}` response indicates that the request is being processed.
  * Later, you can [retrieve the unsubscribed email list](/reference/retrieve-unsubscribed-emails) to see if the emails are removed from the list, or retrieve the user profiles using [this api](/reference/retrieve-users) to make sure the user's `email_unsubscribe` field is now set to `false`.

# Retrieve Mobile App Users

Ask AI

This API method allows you to retrieve data about the app users who installed the app.

Send a GET request to `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/profiles/`.  
A sample call would be:

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
          -H "Authorization: Token <your api token>" \
          -H "appid: <your appid>" \
          "https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/profiles/"

## 

Specifying start and end dates

You can also provide `start_date` and `end_date` parameters to the API call. If these parameters are provided, the API fetches entries only for the users who installed the app on or after the `start_date`, but on or before the `end_date`.

The format for both is `yyyy-mm-dd`. A sample call would be:

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
          -H "Authorization: Token <your api token>" \
          -H "appid: <your appid>" \
          "https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/profiles/?start_date=2015-12-22&end_date=2015-12-25"

For faster response times, retrieve data in small date ranges.

## 

Specifying the OS

You can specify the OS using the query parameter `os`. Supported values are `android` and `ios` (production environment).

  * The default value for `os` is `android`.
  * The value is **case-sensitive**.



Here is an example of using this variable:

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
          -H "Authorization: Token <your api token>" \
          -H "appid: <your appid>" \
          "https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/profiles/?start_date=2015-12-22&end_date=2015-12-25&os=android"

## 

Specifying specific fields to retrieve

The following fields are available via API.

Field Name| Description  
---|---  
`firstSeen`| The date when the user installed your app.  
`email`| The email of the user, if available.  
`uninstallTime`| The date when AIQUA detected that the user has uninstalled your app.  
`user_id`| The user ID set by `setUserId()` function of the SDK.  
`gcmId`| The GCM registration ID of the user (for Android) or device token (for iOS).  
  
You can specify which specific fields you want. For example, if you want to get `firstSeen`, `uninstallTime`, and `gcmId` of all the users who installed your app between April 22, 2019 and April 25, 2019, the relevant curl call would be:

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
          -H "Authorization: Token <your api token>" \
          -H "appid: <your appid>" \
          "https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/profiles/?start_date=2019-04-22&end_date=2019-04-25&fields=firstSeen,uninstallTime,gcmId"

For faster response times, retrieve only the fields that you need.

# Retrieve an Offline Event Segment (v1)

Ask AI

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Deprecation notice
> 
> The Offline Event API v1 is deprecated and is only supported for legacy users. Please use the [Offline Event API v2](/reference/getting-starting-with-the-offline-event-api-v2) to upload offline event data instead.

You can use the following API to retrieve segment information:

  * **Retrieving all source segments** : You can retrieve the segment information for all offline-event source segments by leaving out the segment ID in the endpoint URL. This is useful if you need to look up the segment IDs.
  * **Retrieving a specific segment** : You can specify the segment ID to retrieve an offline-event source or derived segment.



Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| GET  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token `<your api token>`", "appid": "`<your appid>`"}  
URL| Retrieving all source segments:  
<https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/>Retrieving a specific segment:  
[https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/{segment_id}/](https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/%7Bsegment_id%7D/)  
Success Response Code| 200 - OK  
Error Codes| 400 - Bad Request  
404 - Not Found (if the specified segment ID is invalid or not an offline-event segment)  
  
### 

Sample Request

retrieving all source segmentsretrieving a specific segment
    
    
    curl -H 'appid: <your_app_id>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <your_api_token>' \
         https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/
    
    
    curl -H 'appid: <your_app_id>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <your_api_token>' \
         https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/{segment_id}

### 

Sample Response

retrieved all source segmentsretrieved a source segmentretreived a derived segment
    
    
    [
        {
            "id": 26231,
            "type": "offline_event_source",
            "name": "my-offline-event-segment-01",
            "description": "Offline event segment created using API",
            "period": {
                "to": 1585561457,
                "from": 1585561456
            },
            "created": "2020-05-20T08:04:51Z",
            "modified": "2020-05-20T08:27:27Z"
        },
        {
            "id": 28641,

            "name": "my-offline-event-segment-02",
            "description": "Offline event segment created using API",
            "period": {

                "from": 946691746
            },
            "created": "2020-07-15T04:31:31Z",
            "modified": "2020-08-05T03:20:56Z"
        },
    ]
    
    
    {
        "id": 26231,

# Retrieve Push Subscribers

Ask AI

This API method allows you to retrieve your push subscribers. You can specify a platform to fetch the list of `user_id` of your web, Android, or iOS push subscribers. The API returns paginated data with a batch size of 1000.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> The list of push subscribers may include duplicated `user_id` when multiple users have the same `user_id` (the custom user ID used by your company).

## 

Using the API

Each API request returns paginated data with a batch size of 1000. The response contains the `offset` to fetch data for the next page. In the initial request, `offset` should be omitted.

To fetch the data for the next batch, pass the `offset` returned in the previous response.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| GET  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token <your api token>", "appid": "<your appid>"}  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/subscribers/>  
Required Parameters| • `platform`: Specify `ios`, `android`, or `web` (This is case-sensitive).  
• `offset`: Use the offset in the response to retrieve the next batch.  
Response| • {"status": "success", "data": ["U0001", "U0002", "U0003", ...], "offset": "5b5645a6s6446f9321"}• {"status": "success", "data": [], "offset": null}  
Error Codes| • 400 {"status": "failure", "message": "missing/invalid platform as parameter"}• 400 {"status": 'failure", "message": "invalid offset"}  
  
> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip:
> 
> To find the **API Token** and **APP ID** of your account, go to the [Account Settings](https://aiqua.appier.com/settings) page on AIQUA Dashboard.

## 

Sample Request 1

This is a sample request to fetch the first 1000 `web` push subscribers.

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
       'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/subscribers/?platform=web' \
       -H 'Authorization: Token <your token>' \
       -H 'appid: <your app_id>'

#### 

Response

JSON
    
    
    {
        "status": "success",
        "data": [
            "U0001",
            "U0002",
            "U0003",
            ...
        ],
        "offset": "5ec8e357e68090fe4bde27ac"
    }

## 

Sample Request 2

To retrieve the next batch of subscribers, pass the `offset` returned in the previous response.

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
       'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v1/users/subscribers/?platform=web&offset=5ec8e357e68090fe4bde27ac' \
       -H 'Authorization: Token <your token>' \
       -H 'appid: <your app_id>'

#### 

Response

After the final batch of subscribers is retrieved, a `null` value will be returned for `offset` in the next response.

JSON
    
    
    {
        "status": "success",
        "data": [],
        "offset": null
    }

# Retrieve the Status of a Report Job

Ask AI

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Prerequisites
> 
> You need to create a [report job](/reference/create-a-report-job) and obtain a job ID before you can retrieve its status.

## 

Endpoint URL

After [creating a report job](/reference/create-a-report-job) and obtaining a job ID, make a request to the following endpoint to get the report job's status. If the job was successful, a download URL for the report will be included in the response.

HTTP Method| Endpoint URL  
---|---  
**`GET`**| ` https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/{job_id}`  
  
## 

Authorization and headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token: <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
* * *

## 

Request

Replace `{job_id}` with the job ID contained in the response from [creating the report job](/reference/create-a-report-job#response).

JSON
    
    
    curl --location --request GET 'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/reporting/runs/{job_id}' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --header 'Authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
    --header 'appid: <APP_ID>'

* * *

## 

Response

### 

Job status

The `status` field describes the current status of the report job you created, and can have one of the following values:

  * `pending`: The report job hasn't start yet.
  * `running`: The report job is currently in progress.
  * `failed`: The report job failed. If you continue to encounter report job failures, please contact your customer success manager. 
  * `succeed`: The report job successfully ran and generated the requested report. A report download URL will be included in `details.report.url`.



### 

Report details

Details about the generated report can be found under `details.report`, depending on what type of report job you created:

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`details.report.url`| string| The report download URL, valid for 7 days after the creation time (`details.report.created`)  
`details.report.size`| integer| The report's file size in bytes  
`details.report.format`| string| The report's file format  
`details.report.created`| integer| A UNIX timestamp (seconds) indicating the report's creation time  
  
### 

Example response: Campaign performance report

JSON
    
    
    {
        "id": "<JOB_ID>",
        "job_type": "campaign.details",
        "parameters": {
            "format": "csv",
            "campaign_ids": [
                123,
                456, 
                789
            ],
            "campaign_type": "regular"
        },
        "created": "2021-03-30T18:20:13Z",
        "updated": "2021-03-30T18:20:14Z",
        "status": "succeed",
        "details": {
            "report": {
                "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                "size": 1083,
                "format": "csv",
                "object": "file",
                "created": 1617128414
            }
        }
    }

### 

Example response: Campaign user report

JSON
    
    
    {
        "id": "<JOB_ID>",
        "job_type": "campaign.interaction.data",
        "parameters": {
            "dates": {
                "end": 1677225599,
                "start": 1671955371
            },
            "format": "csv",
            "campaign_id": 404100,
            "attribution_type": "last_clicked",
            "campaign_name": "My campaign"
        },
        "created": "2023-02-23T09:39:16Z",
        "updated": "2023-02-23T09:39:41Z",
        "status": "succeed",
        "details": {
            "report": {
                "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                "size": 663, 
                "format": "csv",
                "object": "file",
                "created": 1677145181
            }
        }
    }

### 

Example response: Segment report

The response may contain multiple download URLs, each corresponding to a platform-specific report, i.e. reports for Android, iOS, iOS (dev), SMS, LINE, web, and email. The report will be included if the segment contains at least one user from that platform.

JSON
    
    
    {
        "id": "<JOB_ID>",
        "job_type": "seg.file.generator",
        "parameters": {
            "format": "csv",
            "segment_ids": [
                1234
            ]
        },
        "created": "2023-03-15T07:02:31Z",
        "updated": "2023-03-15T07:04:50Z",
        "status": "succeed",
        "details": {
            "1234": {
                "iOS_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 12171,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863861
                },
                "sms_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 1838591,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863880
                },
                "web_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 99771713,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863859
                },
                "line_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 5896376,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863890
                },
                "email_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 6594152,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863871
                },
                "android_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 15366,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863861
                },
                "iOS(dev)_users": {
                    "url": "<REPORT_DOWNLOAD_URL>",
                    "size": 14517,
                    "format": "csv",
                    "object": "file",
                    "created": 1678863861
                }
            }
        }
    }
