# Retrieve the Unsubscribed Email List

Ask AI

This **`GET`** request returns the list of emails that have been unsubscribed.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| GET  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token <your api token>", "appid": "<your appid>"}  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/?unsubscribed=true>  
Success Response| 

  * 200 OK {"emails": ["[user01@example.com](mailto:user01@example.com)", "[user02@example.com](mailto:user02@example.com)",...]}

  
Error Codes| 

  * 400 Bad Request

  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> It is NOT supported to use `?unsubscribed=false` to retrieve emails that are not on the unsubscribed list.

### 

Sample Request

This is a sample request to retrieve all users who have unsubscribed from the email list.

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
         -H 'appid: <your app_id>' \
         -H 'authorization: Token <your token>' \
         'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/?unsubscribed=true'

### 

Sample Output

JSON
    
    
    {
        "emails": [
            "user01@example.com",
            "user02@example.com",
            "user03@example.com",
            "user04@example.com",
            "user05@example.com",
            "user06@example.com"
        ]
    }

# Retrieve Users

Ask AI

Use this API to retrieve user profiles based on different identifiers and update user profiles of an individual user by specifying `userId` and `platform`.

In countries where marketers need to obtain users' explicit consent before sending marketing campaigns to them, a common use case is to [update the profiles](/reference/update-users) of users who have granted this additional consent. You can use the retrieve API to look up the `userId` of the user profile you want to update.

Retrieving user profiles can also be useful when you are troubleshooting issues for a specific user.

* * *

## 

Retrieving user profiles

This API method allows you to retrieve the profile data of a particular user based on an identifier such as the user's phone number, id, or email.

Send a GET request to `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/`. Each API request returns a JSON which contains an array of found users with their available user profile data.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> This API method is intended for manually retrieving individual user profiles only. Do not use this API method for bulk retrieval of user data as it may interfere with normal data logging operation on AIQUA database.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| `GET`  
Headers| `{"Authorization": "Token ", "appid": ""}`  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/>  
Parameters| Required** (use only one): `user_id`, `phoneNo`, `email`, `IDFA`, `AAID`, `userId`   
Optional: `fields`  
Response| If user profiles retrieved:   
`200 OK`   
`{"users": [{...}, {...}, ...]}`   
  
If no users found:   
`200 OK`   
`{"users": []}`  
Error Codes| 400 Bad Request   
`{"status": "failure", "message": "Please use exactly one identifier to search user"}`   
`{"status": "failure", "message": "Please use one of the supported identifiers (case-sensitive)."} `   
`{"status": "failure", "message": "identifier value cannot be empty."}` <b  
  
  * Your [api token and appid](/reference/aiqua-api-getting-started) can be found in AIQUA's **Account Settings** page.
  * Use one of these identifiers in the query string to search users: `user_id`, `phoneNo`, `email`, `IDFA`, `AAID`, `userId` (AIQUA userId)
  * Use only one identifier at a time.
  * The identifiers are case-sensitive. Use lowercase letters in email addresses.
  * You can retrieve only the profile fields you need by adding `fields` in the query string.
  * Use commas to separate multiple fields
  * If `fields` is not specified, all available fields will be included in the output.

  


### 

Sample request 1:

Search for user profiles with `email` equals to "[jane.doe@email.com](mailto:jane.doe@email.com)".

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
    'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/?email=jane.doe@email.com' \
    -H 'authorization: Token <your token>' \
    -H 'appid: <your app_id>'

Sample response:

JSON
    
    
    {
    "users": [
        {
            "userId": 6362562705371523072,
            "aiq_email_unsub_hmac": "$1$c7df1600957bd1a51d86e9aefb03a39db8f979fb2ff11b3bed847d74106c62d7",
            "aiq_push_enabled": true,
            "email": "jane.doe@email.com",
            "ip": "119.14.219.83",
            "mTime": 1597786228,
            "name": "Jane Doe",
            "phoneNo": "+886912345678",
            "qgFcm": true,
            "sdkVersion": "6.0.1",
            "work_email": "jane.doe@company.com",
            "platform": "android"
        },
        {
            "userId": 1001109948875897228,
            "aiq_email_unsub_hmac": "$1$c7df1600957bd1a51d86e9aefb03a39db8f979fb2ff11b3bed847d74106c62d7",
            "aiq_merge_status": 5,
            "email": "jane.doe@email.com",
            "fromApi": true,
            "mTime": 1597720493,
            "name": "Jane Doe",
            "last_name": "Doe",
            "phoneNo": "+886912345678",
            "first_name": "Jane",
            "platform": "offline"
        }
    ]
    }

  


### 

Sample request 2:

Search for user profiles with `phoneNo` equals to "0912345678" and include only `user_id` and `aiq_push_enabled` fields in the output.

cURL
    
    
    curl -X GET \
    'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/?phoneNo=0912345678&fields=user_id,aiq_push_enabled' \
    -H 'authorization: Token <your token>' \
    -H 'appid: <your app_id>'

Sample response:

JSON
    
    
    {
    "users": [
        {
            "user_id": "987654321",
            "aiq_push_enabled": true
        }
    ]
    }

# Send Campaign Notifications

Ask AI

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
**`POST`**| ` https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/send-notification/`  
  
* * *

## 

Rate limits

This endpoint is rate-limited to 1800 requests per minute. If this limit is exceeded, you'll receive the following **`429 Too Many Requests`** response:

429 Too Many Requests
    
    
    {
      "message": "API rate limit exceeded",
      "request_id": "36a62679af8eeb0c6491e232f9e0af2e"
    }

To view the remaining number of requests you can make, refer to the following fields in the response head:

  * `ratelimit-remaining`: The remaining number of requests allowed for the current time window.
  * `ratelimit-reset`: The time (in seconds) remaining in the current time window. After this time has elapsed, `ratelimit-remaining` will be reset to 1800.



* * *

## 

Body parameters

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`cid`| String| Required. The ID of the campaign to deliver.  
`message`| Object| Required. The campaign content. For examples of `message` for different creative types, see `message` formats.  
`os`| String| Required for Android, iOS, and web campaigns. Must be one of the following values:  
• `"android"`  
• `"ios-prod"`  
• `"web"`  
(Choose one of the following)  
• `registration_ids`  
• `user_ids`  
• `emails`  
• `phone_nos`  
| Array or String| Required. Specify who to send the campaign to. Only include one of the following:  
  
• `registration_ids`: An array of up to 5,000 FCM tokens (Android) or device tokens (iOS). Supported for the following `os` values: `"android"`, `"ios-prod"`.  
  
• `user_ids`: An array of up to 5,000 values of `user_id`. Supported for all values of `os`.  
  
• `emails`: An array of up to 5,000 email addresses. Supported for all values of `os`.  
  
• `phone_nos`: An array of up to 5,000 phone numbers. The specified phone numbers must have previously been uploaded via the [Bulk Upload Offline Users API](/reference/bulk-upload-offline-users) or logged via Appier SDK using the `phoneNo` attribute for the corresponding user to receive the SMS notification. Supported when `channel` is `"sms"`.  
  
  
`channel`| String| Only required if sending SMS campaigns. Must be `"sms"`.  
  
* * *

## 

Limitations

### 

Push campaigns

  1. The API only supports legacy regular push campaigns for a single channel. If your interface matches the provided screenshot, this indicates you are using a merged push campaign, which cannot be sent via the API.

![](https://files.readme.io/e9e4f419df5ffd4a99554be44343dc0ca2fa2a175fba09fd6ee6923d08469b22-message-push.png)

  2. To send merged push campaigns (multi-channel), send the campaign on the AIQUA dashboard instead.



* * *

## 

Prerequisites

Send campaign notifications to your users by calling this function in the AIQUA API. Refer to the following steps:

### 

1\. Create a campaign on the AIQUA dashboard

First, you need to create a campaign. Go to **Campaigns** , select the campaign you'd like to create, and click the **Create New Campaign**. You can fill in any value in the campaign. They will be overridden by what you provided in the API call.

### 

2\. Retrieve the campaign ID

Edit the campaign you created. The campaign ID is visible in the last portion of the page URL.

![](https://files.readme.io/b76d0bf-campaign-id.png)

* * *

## 

Sample request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X 'POST' https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/send-notification/ --header 'appid: <YOUR_APP_ID>' \
     --header 'Authorization: Token <YOUR_API_TOKEN>' -H 'accept: application/json' -H 'content-type: application/json' \
     -d '
     {
       "cid": 123,
       "phone_nos": ["11111111", "22222222"]
       "message": {"message": "This is an SMS message"},
       "channel":"sms"
     }'

* * *

## 

Sample response

cURL
    
    
    {"status":"accepted","message_id":"12345"}

* * *

## 

`message` formats

  * SMS notification
  * Standard Notification
  * Banner notification
  * Carousel notification
  * Slider notification
  * Animated banner notification



### 

SMS notification

SMS message
    
    
    {
      "message": "This is an SMS message."
    }

### 

Standard Notification

#### 

Android Standard Notification

Android - Standard
    
    
    {
        "type": "basic",
        "title": <title of the notification>,
        "message": <body of the notification>,
        "imageUrl": <url of the icon image> (optional),
        "bigImageUrl": <url of the big image> (optional),
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification> (optional)
        "actions": [{"id": 1, "text": "<button 1 text>", "deepLink": "<deep link if any>"},
                    {"id": 2, "text": "<button 2 text>", "deepLink": "<deep link if any>"},
                    {"id": 3, "text": "<button 3 text>", "deepLink": "<deep link if any>"}] <optional>
    }

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note:
> 
> If you'd like a campaign with action buttons to become a poll campaign (where, upon pressing the button, user response is recorded without opening the app), set the key poll to true in the message. You can send 1, 2, or 3 actions, and adding a deep link within each button is optional.

#### 

iOS Standard Notification

iOS - Standard
    
    
    {
      "type": "basic",
      "aps": {
        "alert": {
          "title": <title of the notification>,
          "body": <body of the notification>,
          "subtitle": <subtitle of the notification>(optional)
        },
        "sound": <custom sound filename>(optional)
      },
      "deepLink": <deep link> (optional),
      "mediaUrl": <media attachment url: image/audio/video/gif>(optional)
    }

#### 

Web Standard Notification

Web - Standard
    
    
    {
      "type": "web",
      "title": "<title of the notification>",
      "body": "<body of the notification>",
      "icon": "<url of the icon image>",
      "image": "<url of the big image>", (optional)
      "data": {
        "url": "<destination url of notification>", (optional)
      },
      "actions": [
        {"title": "<button 1 text>", "url": "<destination url if any>", "icon": "<url of the icon image>"},
        {"title": "<button 2 text>", "url": "<destination url if any>", "icon": "<url of the icon image>"}
      ], (optional)
      "drip": {<same structure with above fields>} (optional)
    }

### 

Banner Notification

Banner notifications are only available on Android devices.

Android - Banner
    
    
    {
        "type": "banner",
        "title": <title of the notification>,
        "message": <body of the notification>,
        "contentImageUrl": <url of banner image>,
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification> (optional)
    }

### 

Carousel Notification

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Important
> 
> `carousel` must contain at least three and up to 10 objects for both Android and iOS.

#### 

Android Carousel Notification

Android - Carousel
    
    
    {
        "type": "carousel",
        "title": <title of the notification>,
        "message": <body of the notification>,
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification> (optional),
        "qg_prev_button":"https://cdn.qgraph.io/img/left.png",
        "qg_next_button":"https://cdn.qgraph.io/img/right.png",
        "carousel": [{"image": "<URL of the image>",
                      "deepLink": "<deep link for image, if any>",
                      "title": "<title of image (optional)>",
                      "message": "<message of image (optional)>"},
                ... you can have up to 10 such elements]
    }

#### 

iOS Carousel Notification

Use `cropImage` to control the image's aspect ratio:

  * `true`: Maintain the original aspect ratio, cropping the image to fit in the container.
  * `false`: Scale the image to fill the whole container rather than scaling to the original aspect ratio.



iOS - Carousel
    
    
    {
        "type": "carousel",
        "carouselType": <Linear, Rotary, Cylinder, CoverFlow>
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification> (optional),
        "aps": {
            "alert": {
                "subtitle": "Carousel Subtitle",
                "body": "Message Body",
                "title": "Carousel Title"
            },
            "sound": <custom sound filename>(optional)
        },
        "carousel": [
            {
              "image": "https://imgurl01.com/a.png",
              "title": "title for the first image",
              "deepLink": <deeplink of the image>(optional),
              "message": <description of the image>(optional)
            },
            {
              "image": "https://imgurl01.com/a.png",
              "title": "title for the second image",
              "deepLink": <deeplink of the image>(optional),
              "message": <description of the image>(optional)
            },
            {
              "image": "https://imgurl01.com/a.png",
              "title": "title for the third image",
              "deepLink": <deeplink of the image>(optional),
              "message": <description of the image>(optional)
            }
            ... you can have up to 10 such elements
        ],
        "cropImage": true (optional - default to false),
    }

### 

Slider Notification

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Important
> 
> `slider` must contain at least two and up to 10 objects for both Android and iOS.

#### 

Android Slider Notification

Android - Slider
    
    
    {
        "type": "slider",
        "title": <title of the notification>,
        "message": <body of the notification>,
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification>, (optional)
        "qg_prev_button":"https://cdn.qgraph.io/img/left.png",
        "qg_next_button":"https://cdn.qgraph.io/img/right.png",
        "slider": [{"image": "<URL of the image>",
                   "deepLink": "<deep link for image, if any>"},
                ... you can have up to 10 such elements]
    }

#### 

iOS Slider Notification

iOS - Slider
    
    
    {
        "type": "slider",
        "carouselType": "Linear",
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification> (optional),
        "aps": {
            "alert": {
                "subtitle": "Carousel Subtitle",
                "body": "Message Body",
                "title": "Carousel Title"
            },
            "sound": <custom sound filename>(optional)          
        },
        "carousel": [
            {
              "image": "https://imgurl01.com/a.png",
              "title": "title for the first image",
              "deepLink": <deeplink of the image>(optional),
              "message": <description of the image>(optional)
            },
            {
              "image": "https://imgurl01.com/a.png",
              "title": "title for the second image",
              "deepLink": <deeplink of the image>(optional),
              "message": <description of the image>(optional)
            },
            {
              "image": "https://imgurl01.com/a.png",
              "title": "title for the third image",
              "deepLink": <deeplink of the image>(optional),
              "message": <description of the image>(optional)
            }
            ... you can have up to 10 such elements
        ],
        "cropImage": true (optional - default to false),
    }

### 

Animated Banner Notification

The following format is only available in Android.

Android - Animated Banner
    
    
    {
        "title": <title of the notification>,
        "message": <body of the notification>,
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification>, (optional)
        "gifPlayButton": "https://cdn.qgraph.io/img/video_button.png",
        "type": "animation"
        "animation": {
            "millisecondsToRefresh": <duration between two frames in milliseconds>,
            "images": [url1, url2, ..., url n]
        }
    }

* * *

## 

Including key-value pairs (Android, iOS)

In addition to the default AIQUA campaign fields such as the title, message, and icon URL, you can send custom data to your app by including key-value pairs in your Android push campaigns.

To include additional key-value pairs, include a `qgPayload` object containing the key-value pairs in `message`. For example, a sample message for Android would be:

Key-value Pairs
    
    
    {
        "type": "basic",
        "title": <title of the notification>,
        "message": <body of the notification>,
        "imageUrl": <url of the icon image> (optional),
        "bigImageUrl": <url of the big image> (optional),
        "deepLink": <deep link of notification> (optional)
        "qgPayload": {
            "key1": "some value",
            "key2": 123
         }
    }

For instructions on extracting custom key-value pairs, refer to the guides for [iOS](/docs/customizing-push-notifications-ios) and [Android](/docs/setting-up-notifications).

* * *

## 

Checking notification status

To check the status of your notifications sent via API, on the AIQUA dashboard, go to **Settings > Notification API**.

# Stream Data to AIQUA

Ask AI

## 

Overview

In addition to logging user events and attributes using the Appier SDKs, you can implement a server-to-server solution using the Streaming API to upload data directly to AIQUA from your server. User data uploaded through this API can be used for:

  * [Segmentation](/docs/segments-create-conditions) conditions (supported for all campaign types)
  * [Trigger rules](/docs/campaigns-trigger-create#3-select-your-trigger-rule) for trigger campaigns
  * Training data for [Recommendation models](/docs/recommendations-v2)



### 

Limitations

  * The request payload must not exceed 10 KB
  * Request payloads with non-ASCII characters must be UTF-8 encoded
  * Each request can only upload data for a single user
  * User events uploaded via the Streaming API can't be used for: 
    * In-web and in-app campaign trigger rules, including in-web and in-app campaigns in journey maps.
    * Event conversion and attribution. These user events cannot be attributed to AIQUA campaigns.



* * *

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
**`POST`**| ` https://api.quantumgraph.com/qga/clients-data/`  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> We suggest streaming data at a rate that doesn't exceed 50 requests per second.

## 

Headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Content-Type`| `application/json`  
  
## 

Body parameters

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`appId`| string| Required. Your app ID. Retrieve this value in the **Account Settings** page.  
`appSecret`| string| Required. Your app secret. Retrieve this value in the **Account Settings** page.  
`identifier`| string| Required. The user's unique identifier. Accepted values: `user_id`, `email`, `phoneNo`, `IDFA`, `advertisingId`.AIQUA will try to map streamed user data with existing Android, iOS, or Web users in the database based on the `identifier` you select. If you're using `user_id`, `email`, or `phoneNo`, ensure that this data is being logged using the Appier SDK.  
`identifier_value`| string| Required. The value of the identifier.  
`device`| string| Required. The user's device type. Accepted values: `"ios"` (iOS production), `"android"`, `"web"`.Note: Data streaming for iOS development devices isn't supported.  
`profiles`| Profile| Optional. Aprofile objectcontaining user attribute data.  
`events`| Event[]| Optional. A list ofevent objectscontaining user event data.  
  
### 

Profile object

The profile object contains the user attributes you'd like to upload. For example, a profile object might look like this:

JSON
    
    
    {
        "first_name": "John",
        "last_name": "Doe"
    }

### 

Event object

An event object contains user event data, and consists of the following fields:

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`eventName`| string| Required. The name of the event.  
`parameters`| object| Optional. An object containing event parameters.  
  
#### 

Example event object (user event without parameters)

JSON
    
    
    {
      "eventName": "user_registered"
    }

#### 

Example event object (user event with parameters)

JSON
    
    
    {
      "eventName": "product_purchased",
      "parameters": {
        "order_id": "3j3dkd4k50",
        "product_id": "319-dh",
        "product_price": 99.99
      }
    }

* * *

## 

Example requests

### 

Uploading user attributes and events

JSON
    
    
    curl --location 'https://api.quantumgraph.com/qga/clients-data/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --data '{
       "appId":"<APP_ID>",
       "appSecret":"<APP_SECRET>",
       "identifier":"email",
       "identifier_value":"user@example.com",
       "device":"ios",
       "profiles":{
         "first_name":"John",
         "last_name":"Doe"
       },
       "events":[
         {
           "eventName": "order_placed"
         }
       ]
    }'

### 

Uploading user attributes (no events)

JSON
    
    
    curl --location 'https://api.quantumgraph.com/qga/clients-data/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --data '{
       "appId":"<APP_ID>",
       "appSecret":"<APP_SECRET>",
       "identifier":"email",
       "identifier_value":"user@example.com",
       "device":"ios",
       "profiles":{
         "first_name":"John",
         "last_name":"Doe"
       }
    }'

### 

Uploading user events (no attributes)

JSON
    
    
    curl --location 'https://api.quantumgraph.com/qga/clients-data/' \
    --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
    --data '{
       "appId":"<APP_ID>",
       "appSecret":"<APP_SECRET>",
       "identifier":"email",
       "identifier_value":"user@example.com",
       "device":"ios",
       "events":[
         {
           "eventName": "order_placed"
         }
       ]
    }'

* * *

## 

Status codes

Code| Status| Description  
---|---|---  
`200`| `OK`| Data successfully uploaded.  
`400`| `Bad Request`| • identifier and identifier_value is required • identifier should be either email, IDFA, user_id, advertisingId or phoneNo • At least one of profiles or events is required • Request body is empty • Malformed JSON body given!  
`413`| `Content Too Large`| • Payload size exceeds 10240 bytes limit  
`500`| `Internal Server Error`| • Internal Server Error

# Update a Mapped User List Segment

Ask AI

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> See [Create a User Segment](/reference/create-a-segment#1-prepare-the-segment-file) for segment file requirements.
> 
> To update a user segment, you'll need the value of `filename` returned in the response from [Create a User Segment](/reference/create-a-user-segent).

Make a `PUT` request to `https://aiqua.appier.com/qganalyzedata/upload_segment/` with the following parameters:

Parameter| Description| Required/Optional  
---|---|---  
`name`| The segment's name. The existing name will be overwritten by this value.| Required  
`description`| A description for the segment. The existing description will be overwritten by this value.| Optional  
`filename`| The temporary filename returned in the response from [uploading the segment file](/reference/create-a-user-segment#1-upload-the-segment-file).| Required  
`field`| The field name of the [user identifier](/docs/user-data-collection#user-identifiers) specified in the segment file. For example, `email`, `phoneNo`, or `user_id`.| Required  
`id`| The ID of the segment to update. Included in the response from [uploading the segment file](/reference/create-a-user-segment#1-upload-the-segment-file).| Required  
  
  * `filename`: One of the values returned after uploading the segment file.
  * `field`: The user attribute used in the segment file.
  * `name` and `description`: The name and description inputted here will overwrite the name and description of the existing segment you want to update.
  * `id`: This is the segment ID of the existing segment you want to update. You can find the segment ID in the API response returned previously, or find it on AIQUA dashboard: 
    1. Go to **Audiences > Segment List**.
    2. Select the segment you want to update, and then click the edit button.
    3. In the Audience - Edit Segment page, find the segment ID in the URL.

![1361](https://files.readme.io/3a35cc0-create_user_uploaded_segment_step_3_aiqua_web_ui_edited.png)

Sample Request:

cURL
    
    
    curl -X PUT\
         -H 'Authorization: Token <your token>'\
         -H 'appId: <your app id>' \
         -H 'content-type: application/json'\
         -d '{"id": "<segment ID>", "name": "<segment name>", "description": "<segment description>", "filename": "<temporary filename>", "field": "<user identifer used in the segment file>"}'\ 
         https://aiqua.appier.com/qganalyzedata/upload_segment/

# Update Users

Ask AI

You can use this API to update the profile of an individual user by specifying `userId` and `platform`. To retrieve a user's `userId` and additional attribute data, use [Retrieve Users](/reference/retrieve-users).

## 

Updating user profiles

You can update the profile data of a user by indicating the user's `userId` and `platform` in a PATCH request.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| PATCH  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token <your api token>", "appid": "<your appid>"}  
URL| <https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/>  
Required Parameters| • `userId`  
• `platform` (Supported platform: `android`, `ios`, `web`, or `offline`)  
Success Response Code| • 200 OK {"status": "update in progress"}  
Error Codes| • 400 Bad Request  
• 404 Not Found (User not found on the specified platform)  
  
You must specify the user's `userId` and `platform` in the query string.

  * The `userId` can be found in the API response when you retrieve user profiles.
  * `platform` value can be one of `android`, `ios`, `web`, or `offline`.
  * The identifiers are **case-sensitive**.



The following profile parameters cannot be updated. Do NOT include any of these keys in the update request.

  * Any key that starts with `aiq`
  * Any key that starts with `_`
  * Any of the following keys: `userId`, `gcmId`, `mTime`, `firstUserId`, `active`, `display_language`, `daysActive`, `identified`, `permission`, `sdkVersion`, `installSource`, `installType`, `subscribed`, `subscription`, `referrer_timestamp_seconds`, `referrer`, `wUserId`, `uninstallTime`.



### 

Sample request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X PATCH -d '{"consent_date": "2020-08-01", "consent": "true"}' \
        -H 'appid: <your app_id>' \
        -H 'authorization: Token <your token>' \
        'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/users/?userId=12345&platform=web'

A `{"status": "update in progress"}` response indicates that the update request is being processed. The update operation is asynchronous and it might take a while. You may retrieve the user profiles later to find out whether the update is successful.

# Upload Offline Event Data (v1)

Ask AI

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Deprecation notice
> 
> The Offline Event API v1 is deprecated and is only supported for legacy users. Please use the [Offline Event API v2](/reference/getting-starting-with-the-offline-event-api-v2) to upload offline event data instead.

> 📘
> 
> You need to [create an offline event segment](/reference/create-offline-event-segment) and obtain its `segment_id` before you can upload offline event data.

Create a file that contains your offline events with the proper format, then use the following method to upload the file to the offline event segment.

Name| Value  
---|---  
HTTP Method| **`POST`**  
Headers| {"Authorization": "Token `<your api token>`", "appid": "`<your appid>`"}  
URL| `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/{segment_id}/`  
Success Response Code| **`200 - OK`**  
Error Codes| **`400 - Bad Request`**  
  
## 

Offline Event File Format

  * Each line should contain one entry that is a valid JSON object.
  * Each offline event entry must include a timestamp, `user_id`, an event name, and parameters. 
    * The value for `user_id` and parameter name must be a string.
    * Timestamp must be in epoch format.
    * Timestamp must be after 946684801 (2000/01/01 00:00:01 (UTC+0)) and no later than the upload time.
  * If the `user_id` of the uploaded event does not match with any existing `user_id` in the AIQUA database, the entry will be stored in the database, but cannot be used in segmentation and campaigns until it has been matched with a user.
  * The JSON file to be uploaded is limited to 50 MB.



## 

Sample Input Data File

2020Q1_data.txt
    
    
    { "timestamp": 1585561457, "user_id": "uid_1234", "event_name": "product_purchased", "parameters": { "product_name": "leather shoes", "product_id": "12345" , "product_price": "49.99", "store": "taipei_111"}} 
    { "timestamp": 1585563269, "user_id": "uid_1972", "event_name": "member_signup", "parameters": { "newsletter": "on", "store": "taipei_114"}}

## 

Sample Request

cURL
    
    
    $ curl -F offline-event=@2020Q1_data.txt \
        -H 'appid: <your_app_id>' \
        -H 'authorization: Token <your_api_token>' \
        https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v3/offline-event-segments/000/

  * `2020Q1_data.txt` is the name of the file to be uploaded
  * `your_app_id` and `your_api_token` can be found on your AIQUA Dashboard's settings page.
  * `000` is the ID of the segment that the event data will be uploaded to. This is the value of `id` returned in the response from [creating an offline event segment](/reference/create-offline-event-segment).



## 

Sample Response

JSON
    
    
    {
        "id": 29025,
        "valid_records": 98,
        "period": {
            "from": 1585561457,
            "to": 1585561457
        },
        "invalid_line_nums": [
            3,
            5
        ]
    }

  * `valid_records` is the number of offline events successfully uploaded.
  * `period` is the maximum and minimum timestamps of the events in this offline-event source segment.
  * `invalid_line_nums` lists the lines of offline events that failed to upload. This may be due to invalid timestamps, or missing `user_id`. In the sample response above, line 3 and line 5 are invalid.

# Upload Offline Event Data (v2)

Ask AI

Upload a file containing offline event data.

## 

Endpoint URL

Operation| URL  
---|---  
`POST`| `https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events/upload`  
  
## 

Rate limits

Please note the following rate limits when making requests to this endpoint using the same app ID:

  * 2 requests per second
  * 50 requests per minute
  * 100 requests per day



If the rate limit is exceeded, this endpoint returns a **`429 Too Many Requests`** response.

## 

Authorization and headers

Name| Value  
---|---  
`Authorization`| `Token <API_TOKEN>`  
`appid`| `<APP_ID>`  
  
## 

Query parameters

Name| Type| Description  
---|---|---  
`source`| string| Must be `"offline_data"`.  
`aiqua_segment_id`| string| Only required for offline event segmentation. The ID of the [offline segment source](/reference/create-offline-segment-source-v2). If included, the uploaded data can be used to [segment by offline events](/docs/segments-create-offline-events).  
  
## 

Offline event file

The offline event file contains a list of event entries, where each event entry contains details about an offline event.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Offline event attribution
> 
> Offline event attributions are calculated once a day using offline events with a timestamp within the last 90 days.
> 
>   * Since offline attributions are calculated once a day, offline attribution metrics may not appear in the campaign list, campaign performance page, or campaign performance reports immediately
>   * If you upload event data older than 90 days, it won't be included in offline attribution calculations
> 


### 

Event file requirements

  * Must be a UTF-8 TXT file containing [NDJSON](https://github.com/ndjson/ndjson-spec) data not exceeding 50 MB.
  * Each line should contain one event entry that is a valid JSON object containing parameters defined in the offline event schema.
  * Each event entry must contain the required event entry fields.
  * The timestamp range of the event file can't exceed 1000 days, i.e. the difference between the earliest and latest timestamp in the file can't be more than 1000 days.



### 

Event entry fields

Each event entry must contain all required fields and adhere to the requirements listed in the table below, otherwise it will be considered invalid and fail to upload.

For example, an event is considered invalid if `timestamp` is set to a value later than the upload time or `identifier_value` exceeds the 128-character length limit.

Name| Description  
---|---  
`identifier_key`| Required. The key used to uniquely identify users. One of: `user_id`, `email`, `phone_no`, `line_uid`, `aaid`, `idfa`, `idfv`, `dmpid`.If the event identifier doesn't match any value in the AIQUA database, the entry will be uploaded, but can't be used for segmentation or attribution until it has been matched with a user.Note: For an event entry to be used in offline event segmentation, the value of `identifier_key` must be `user_id`. Otherwise, that event can't be used for segmentation.  
`identifier_value`| Required. The value of the user identifier. Must not exceed 128 characters.  
`event_name`| Required. The name of the event. Must conform to the[custom event naming guidelines](/docs/custom-user-data#field-names-for-custom-data), matching the following regular expression: `[a-z\_][a-z0-9_]*`.  
`timestamp`| Required. A UNIX timestamp (seconds) indicating the time the event occurred. Must be after `946684801` (2000/01/01 00:00:01 (UTC+0)) and no later than the event file's upload time.  
`vts`| Optional. The monetary value associated with the event, e.g. the price of the product in a `product_purchased` event.  
`parameters`| Required. An object matching the[offline event schema](/reference/create-offline-event-schema-v2)you created.  
  
### 

Example offline event file

offline_event_data_file.txt
    
    
    { "timestamp": 1585561457, "identifier_key": "user_id", "identifier_value": "uid_1234", "event_name": "product_purchased", "vts": 49.99, "parameters": { "product_name": "leather shoes", "product_id": "12345" , "product_price": "49.99", "store": "taipei_111"}} 
    { "timestamp": 1585563269, "identifier_key": "user_id", "identifier_value": "uid_4321", "event_name": "member_signup", "vts": 0, "parameters": { "newsletter": "on", "store": "taipei_114"}}

## 

Example request

cURL
    
    
    curl -X 'POST' \
      'https://api.enterprise.appier.net/api/v2/offline-events/upload?source=offline_data&aiqua_segment_id=<OFFLINE_SEGMENT_SOURCE_ID>' \
      -H 'appid: <APP_ID>' \
      -H 'authorization: Token <API_TOKEN>' \
      -H 'accept: application/json' \
      -H 'Content-Type: multipart/form-data' \
      -F 'offlineEvent=@offline_event_data_file.txt;type=text/plain'

## 

Example response

200 OK
    
    
    {
       "metadata": {
           "code": "success",
           "reason": "",
           "requestId": "irPZa5E2Se5ExG8gabzqCS"
       },
       "data": {
           "uploadID": "cf9c5ff2-53d2-498b-99cc-f0d4de98b37a",
           "period": {
               "from": 1462380000,
               "to": 1462391103
           },
           "validCount": 2,
           "invalidCount": 0,
           "invalidLineNumbers": []
       }
    }

  * `validCount`: The number of valid events uploaded. Duplicate events (i.e. sharing the same timestamp, identifier value, event name, and event parameters) are deduplicated in the database, but are counted individually in `validCount`.
  * `invalidCount`: The number of invalid events that weren't uploaded.
  * `invalidLineNumbers`: The line numbers of the invalid events.
